Op fusion concept:
We can precompute combined weights for projections? For each row (i,k pair) and channel c, need:
- x_norm (z? call d) after LayerNorm row of length C: x_hat = (x - mean)/std * w + b.
- left pre: Lp = Wl @ x_hat (Z)
- right pre: Rp = Wr @ x_hat
- gates: lg = sigmoid(Wlg @ x_hat), rg = sigmoid(Wrg @ x_hat), og = sigmoid(Wog @ x_hat)
- masked gated: A = Lp * mask_i_k * lg, B = Rp * mask_j_k * rg.
Need compute out[i,j,d] = sum_k A[i,k,d] * B[j,k,d]. Then LN over d, gate og, Wout.

Let's design exact projection kernel for C=128 and C=384 as one `proj_kernel` with branches. Need use `C: tl.constexpr`. Since C=384 not power, but branch uses only 128 aranges.

Pseudo:
```
@triton.jit
def proj_kernel(x_ptr, mask_ptr, A_ptr, B_ptr, OG_ptr, nw_ptr, nb_ptr,
                wl_ptr, wlg_ptr, wr_ptr, wrg_ptr, wog_ptr,
                M, C: tl.constexpr, Z: tl.constexpr,
                BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr, PREC: tl.constexpr):
    pid_b = tl.program_id(0)
    pid_m = tl.program_id(1)
    offs_m = pid_m*BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    m_mask = offs_m < M
    x_base = x_ptr + (pid_b * M + offs_m)[:, None] * C
    c0 = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    z = tl.arange(0, Z)
    if HAS_MASK:
       mask_vals = tl.load(mask_ptr + pid_b*M + offs_m, mask=m_mask, other=0).to(tl.float32)
       mb = mask_vals[:, None]
    else:
       mb = 1.0  # but need shape? scalar works broadcast
    if C == BLOCK_C:
       x0 = tl.load(x_base + c0[None,:], mask=m_mask[:,None], other=0.).to(tl.float32)
       mean = tl.sum(x0,1)/C
       diff0 = x0 - mean[:,None]
       var = tl.sum(diff0*diff0,1)/C
       rstd = 1/tl.sqrt(var+1e-5)
       rw0 = tl.load(nw_ptr + c0)
       rb0 = tl.load(nb_ptr + c0)
       xh0 = (diff0*rstd[:,None]*rw0[None,:] + rb0[None,:]).to(tl.float16)
       # left pre
       w0 = tl.load(wl_ptr + c0[:,None]*Z + z[None,:])
       lp = tl.dot(xh0, w0, input_precision=None?) # fp16 no need
       w0 = tl.load(wlg_ptr + ...)
       lg = tl.dot(xh0, w0)
       a = lp * mb * tl.sigmoid(lg)
       # store A
       a_ptrs = A_ptr + pid_b*Z*M + z[:,None]*M + offs_m[None,:]
       tl.store(a_ptrs, a.to(tl.float16), mask=(offs_m < M)[None,:]) # Need mask shape [Z,BM], bcast
       ...

@triton.jit
def proj_pad_kernel(x_ptr, mask_ptr, A_ptr, B_ptr, OG_ptr, nw_ptr, nb_ptr, weights..., M, C:tl.constexpr, Z:tl.constexpr, BLOCK_M:tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C:tl.constexpr, HAS_MASK:tl.constexpr):

   rw = tl.load(nw_ptr + c, mask=c_mask, other=0)
   rb = ...
   xh = (diff*rstd*rw + rb).to(fp16)

   # two accs etc.

Weight pad function:
```
def _to_fp16_mat(w, shape): # expected maybe w shape [out,in], returns [in, out] fp16 contig, optionally pad to shape (in_pad,out)
   w = w.detach().float()
   w = torch.clamp(w, -65500.0, 65500.0)
   w = w.t().contiguous()
   if w.shape[0] == shape[0] and w.shape[1] == shape[1]:
       return w.to(torch.float16)
   out = torch.zeros(shape, dtype=torch.float16, device=w.device)
   out[:w.shape[0], :w.shape[1]] = w.to(torch.float16)
   return out
```

@triton.jit
def dot_xh1(xh, w_ptr, Z: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr):
    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C); z=tl.arange(0,Z)
    w = tl.load(w_ptr + c[:,None]*Z + z[None,:])
    return tl.dot(xh, w)

@triton.jit
def dot_xh2(xh0, xh1, w_ptr, Z: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr):
   c = tl.arange(0,BLOCK_C); z=tl.arange(0,Z)
   w0 = tl.load(w_ptr + c[:,None]*Z + z[None,:])
   acc = tl.dot(xh0,w0)
   w1 = tl.load(w_ptr + (BLOCK_C+c)[:,None]*Z + z[None,:])
   acc += tl.dot(xh1,w1)
   return acc

@triton.jit
def dot_xh3(...)
```

Let's write final kernel variable names carefully.

```
@triton.jit
def final_kernel(out_ptr, og_ptr, out_w_ptr, out_b_ptr, lw_ptr, lb_ptr,
                 out_final_ptr, M, C_OUT: tl.constexpr, Z: tl.constexpr,
                 BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr):
    pid_b = tl.program_id(0)
    pid_m = tl.program_id(1)
    offs_m = pid_m*BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    m_mask = offs_m < M
    offs_d = tl.arange(0, Z)
    # out_t [Z,BM]
    d_vals = offs_d[:, None]
    m_vals = offs_m[None, :]
    base = out_ptr + pid_b * Z * M
    ptrs = base + d_vals * M + m_vals
    out_t = tl.load(ptrs, mask=m_mask[None, :], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    mean = tl.sum(out_t, axis=0) / Z
    diff = out_t - mean[None, :]
    var = tl.sum(diff * diff, axis=0) / Z
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)
    lw = tl.load(lw_ptr + offs_d)  # [Z]
    lb = tl.load(lb_ptr + offs_d)
    og_t = tl.load(og_ptr + pid_b*Z*M + d_vals*M + m_vals, mask=m_mask[None,:], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    norm_t = diff * rstd[None, :] * lw[:, None] + lb[:, None]
    v_t = (norm_t * og_t).to(tl.float16)
    v = tl.trans(v_t)  # [BM, Z]
    # c loop
    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)  # BLOCK_C=128
    for c0 in range(0, C_OUT, BLOCK_C):
        w = tl.load(out_w_ptr + offs_d[:, None] * C_OUT + (c0 + c)[None, :])  # [Z,128]
        y = tl.dot(v, w)
        out_ptrs = out_final_ptr + (pid_b * M + offs_m)[:, None] * C_OUT + (c0 + c)[None, :]
        tl.store(out_ptrs, y, mask=m_mask[:, None])
```
Need `C_OUT` multiple of BLOCK_C; `BLOCK_C=128`. If C_OUT=384, c0 range(0,384,128). In Triton, `range(0, C_OUT, BLOCK_C)` with C_OUT constexpr? If C_OUT runtime? We pass constexpr. Good.
But `c0 + c` where c0 loop variable int, c tensor [128]. Good.

`tl.load(out_w_ptr + offs_d[:, None] * C_OUT + (c0 + c)[None, :])`: Wout [Z,C] row d, col c. Stride d = C. Good. C could be 384. c0=256, c 0..127 => 256..383. Good.

Need if BLOCK_C maybe 128 and C_OUT=128. Good.

Let's detail host weight conversion.

```
def _as_fp16_t(w, in_pad=None, out=None, device=None):
    w = w.detach()
    if w.dtype != torch.float32:
        w = w.float()
    # clamp to avoid inf in fp16
    maxv = 65500.0
    w = torch.clamp(w, -maxv, maxv)
    w = w.t().contiguous()
    if in_pad is not None and out is not None:
       shape = (in_pad, out)
    else:
       shape = w.shape
    if w.shape == shape:
       return w.to(torch.float16)
    res = torch.zeros(shape, dtype=torch.float16, device=w.device)
    r = min(w.shape[0], shape[0]); c = min(w.shape[1], shape[1])
    res[:r,:c] = w[:r,:c].to(torch.float16)
    return res
```

@triton.jit
def dot1(xh, w_ptr, Z: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr):
    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    z = tl.arange(0, Z)
    w = tl.load(w_ptr + c[:, None] * Z + z[None, :])
    acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, Z), dtype=tl.float32)
    return tl.dot(xh, w, acc)
```

    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    x = tl.load(x_base + c[None, :], mask=m_mask[:, None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    mean = tl.sum(x, axis=1) / C
    diff = x - mean[:, None]
    var = tl.sum(diff * diff, axis=1) / C
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)
    rw = tl.load(nw_ptr + c)
    rb = tl.load(nb_ptr + c)
    xh0 = (diff * rstd[:, None] * rw[None, :] + rb[None, :]).to(tl.float16)

Let's write helper functions carefully with constexpr.

```
@triton.jit
def dot1(xh, w_ptr, Z: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr):
    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    z = tl.arange(0, Z)
    w = tl.load(w_ptr + c[:, None] * Z + z[None, :])
    acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, Z), dtype=tl.float32)
    return tl.dot(xh, w, acc)

@triton.jit
def dot2(xh0, xh1, w_ptr, Z: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr):
    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    z = tl.arange(0, Z)
    w0 = tl.load(w_ptr + c[:, None] * Z + z[None, :])
    acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, Z), dtype=tl.float32)
    acc = tl.dot(xh0, w0, acc)
    w1 = tl.load(w_ptr + (BLOCK_C + c)[:, None] * Z + z[None, :])
    acc = tl.dot(xh1, w1, acc)
    return acc

@triton.jit
def dot3(...)
```
For `dot3`, `2*BLOCK_C + c`.

In pad kernel, `dot_pad` similar but one load full BLOCK_C.

```
@triton.jit
def proj_kernel(...):
    pid_b = tl.program_id(0)
    pid_m = tl.program_id(1)
    offs_m = pid_m * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    m_mask = offs_m < M
    x_base = x_ptr + (pid_b * M + offs_m)[:, None] * C
    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    z = tl.arange(0, Z)
    base = pid_b * Z * M
    store_mask = (offs_m < M)[None, :]
    if HAS_MASK:
        mask_vec = tl.load(mask_ptr + pid_b * M + offs_m, mask=m_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        mask_b = mask_vec[:, None]
    else:
        mask_b = 1.0
    if C == BLOCK_C:
        x0 = tl.load(x_base + c[None, :], mask=m_mask[:, None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        mean = tl.sum(x0, axis=1) / C
        d0 = x0 - mean[:, None]
        var = tl.sum(d0 * d0, axis=1) / C
        rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)
        rw = tl.load(nw_ptr + c)
        rb = tl.load(nb_ptr + c)
        xh0 = (d0 * rstd[:, None] * rw[None, :] + rb[None, :]).to(tl.float16)
        lp = dot1(xh0, wl_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M)
        lg = dot1(xh0, wlg_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M)
        store_gated(lp, lg, A_ptr, base, M, Z, z, offs_m, store_mask, mask_b)
        rp = dot1(xh0, wr_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M)
        rg = dot1(xh0, wrg_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M)
        store_gated(rp, rg, B_ptr, base, M, Z, z, offs_m, store_mask, mask_b)
        og = dot1(xh0, wog_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M)
        store_sigmoid(og, OG_ptr, base, M, Z, z, offs_m, store_mask)
    elif C == 2*BLOCK_C:
        x0 = load c
        x1 = load BLOCK_C+c
        mean = (sum0+sum1)/C
        d0,d1
        var = (sum d0^2 + d1^2)/C
        rstd
        load rw0/rw1 rb0/rb1
        xh0,xh1
        lp=dot2(...), lg=dot2, store; rp=dot2,...; og=dot2
    elif C == 3*BLOCK_C:
        x0,x1,x2
        ...
        lp=dot3,...
```
If C not handled (e.g., fallback pad) no.

Need `C` int, `BLOCK_C` int. `C == 2*BLOCK_C` in constexpr? yes.

Now, `dot1` returns fp32 [BM,Z]. `store_gated` computes sigmoid and trans. Inside store_gated, `mask_b` could be scalar float or tensor [BM,1]. If scalar, `pre * mask_b` works. But in JIT function, `mask_b` might be union type? Triton control flow with a scalar vs tensor in different branches of HAS_MASK? It's constexpr branch, so in compilation one path. For HAS_MASK false, mask_b type fp32 scalar; true tensor. Function called after branch but in two separate compiled versions? Since HAS_MASK constexpr, the whole kernel specializes, type known. Now, final kernel coalescing: Use [Z,BM] loads. Need store output with m_mask.

```
@triton.jit
def final_kernel(out_ptr, og_ptr, w_ptr, lw_ptr, lb_ptr, y_ptr, M, C_OUT: tl.constexpr, Z: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr):
    pid_b = tl.program_id(0)
    pid_m = tl.program_id(1)
    offs_m = pid_m*BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0,BLOCK_M)
    m_mask = offs_m < M
    offs_d = tl.arange(0,Z)
    d_vals = offs_d[:,None]
    m_vals = offs_m[None,:]
    base = pid_b * Z * M
    out_t = tl.load(out_ptr + base + d_vals*M + m_vals, mask=m_mask[None,:], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    mean = tl.sum(out_t, axis=0) / Z
    diff = out_t - mean[None,:]
    var = tl.sum(diff*diff, axis=0) / Z
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)
    lw = tl.load(lw_ptr + offs_d)
    lb = tl.load(lb_ptr + offs_d)
    og_t = tl.load(og_ptr + base + d_vals*M + m_vals, mask=m_mask[None,:], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    norm_t = diff * rstd[None,:] * lw[:,None] + lb[:,None]
    v = (norm_t * og_t).to(tl.float16)
    v_token = tl.trans(v)
    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    for c0 in range(0, C_OUT, BLOCK_C):
        w = tl.load(w_ptr + offs_d[:, None] * C_OUT + (c0 + c)[None, :])
        acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C), dtype=tl.float32)
        y = tl.dot(v_token, w, acc)
        y_ptrs = y_ptr + (pid_b * M + offs_m)[:, None] * C_OUT + (c0 + c)[None, :]
        tl.store(y_ptrs, y, mask=m_mask[:, None])
```
Need if C_OUT not multiple BLOCK_C? assumed. If C_OUT=128, loop once. If 384, three.
Potential register: out_t, diff, v, v_token, og_t. `v_token = tl.trans(v)` maybe keeps both? It might alias. Fine.
Could cast `v` to fp16 before trans? `v_t` [Z,BM], trans. Good.

Maybe we can avoid `og_t` fp32 by keeping fp16? Multiplying norm fp32 by fp16 cast. Fine.

Now, could we use `tl.dot` with `v_token` [BM,Z] fp16 and w [Z,C] fp16. Good.

@triton.jit
def dot1(xh, w_ptr, Z: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr):
    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    z = tl.arange(0, Z)
    w = tl.load(w_ptr + c[:, None] * Z + z[None, :])
    acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, Z), dtype=tl.float32)
    return tl.dot(xh, w, acc)
```
`xh` shape [BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C]. Good. `acc` shape [BLOCK_M,Z]. `tl.dot` with acc returns acc. Good.

`store_gated`:
```
@triton.jit
def store_gated(pre, gate, out_ptr, base, M, Z: tl.constexpr, z, offs_m, store_mask, mask_b):
    val = pre * mask_b * (1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-gate)))
    ptrs = out_ptr + base * Z * M + z[:, None] * M + offs_m[None, :]
    tl.store(ptrs, tl.trans(val).to(tl.float16), mask=store_mask)
```

Now, `_trans_weight_fp16` padding: If we request shape (in_pad, out), but original w.t shape [in,out]. If original out > shape out? not. For to_out pad columns, shape out=C_BLOCK bigger than original C; original out smaller. Code pads. Good.

Implement `_trans_to_fp16`:
```
def _trans_to_fp16(w, shape=None):
    w = w.detach()
    if w.dtype != torch.float32:
       w = w.float()
    maxv = 65500.0
    w = torch.clamp(w, -maxv, maxv)
    w = w.t().contiguous()
    if shape is None:
       return w.to(torch.float16)
    if w.shape == shape:
       return w.to(torch.float16)
    res = torch.zeros(shape, dtype=torch.float16, device=w.device)
    r0 = min(w.shape[0], shape[0]); c0 = min(w.shape[1], shape[1])
    res[:r0, :c0] = w[:r0, :c0].to(torch.float16)
    return res
```
Potential performance: `torch.clamp` on CPU? no. `w.t().contiguous()` after clamp. Good.

If w is already contiguous [out,in], `w.t()` non-contig. `.contiguous()` copy. `.to(torch.float16)` copies. Could combine `w.t().contiguous().to(torch.float16)` maybe one copy? It has to allocate fp16 contiguous. Fine.

Now, contraction kernel.

```
@triton.jit
def contract_kernel(a_ptr, b_ptr, c_ptr, M, N, BLOCK_I: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_J: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_K: tl.constexpr, EVEN: tl.constexpr):
    pid_i = tl.program_id(0)
    pid_j = tl.program_id(1)
    pid_d = tl.program_id(2)
    offs_i = pid_i*BLOCK_I + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_I)
    offs_j = pid_j*BLOCK_J + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_J)
    offs_k = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_K)
    base = pid_d * M
    if EVEN:
        a_ptrs = a_ptr + base + offs_i[:, None] * N + offs_k[None, :]
        b_ptrs = b_ptr + base + offs_j[:, None] * N + offs_k[None, :]
        acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_I, BLOCK_J), dtype=tl.float32)
        for _ in range(0, tl.cdiv(N, BLOCK_K)):
            a = tl.load(a_ptrs)
            b = tl.load(b_ptrs)
            acc = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(b), acc)
            a_ptrs += BLOCK_K
            b_ptrs += BLOCK_K
        c_ptrs = c_ptr + base + offs_i[:, None] * N + offs_j[None, :]
        tl.store(c_ptrs, acc)
    else:
        i_mask = offs_i < N
        j_mask = offs_j < N
        acc = tl.zeros(...)
        for k in range(0, tl.cdiv(N, BLOCK_K)):
           kk = k*BLOCK_K + offs_k
           k_mask = kk < N
           amask = i_mask[:,None] & k_mask[None,:]
           bmask = j_mask[:,None] & k_mask[None,:]
           a = tl.load(a_ptr + base + offs_i[:,None]*N + kk[None,:], mask=amask, other=0.)
           b = ...
           acc = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(b), acc)
        c_ptrs = ...
        tl.store(c_ptrs, acc, mask=i_mask[:,None] & j_mask[None,:])
```
In EVEN, `base = pid_d * M`. If `pid_d` int32 and M int32, product maybe. Good. `N` runtime. `tl.cdiv(N,BLOCK_K)` returns int. If EVEN true, N divisible, but cdiv okay.
Potential issue: Pointer update `a_ptrs += BLOCK_K` where `a_ptrs` is a tensor of pointers. Adding int increments all pointers by BLOCK_K. Correct because k column offset increases. Good.

In masked branch, inside loop recompute full pointer each iter. Good.

Now, final kernel with C_NEEDS_MASK.

```
@triton.jit
def final_kernel(out_ptr, og_ptr, w_ptr, lw_ptr, lb_ptr, y_ptr, M, C_OUT: tl.constexpr, C_STRIDE: tl.constexpr, Z: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, C_NEEDS_MASK: tl.constexpr):
    pid_b = tl.program_id(0)
    pid_m = tl.program_id(1)
    offs_m = pid_m*BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    m_mask = offs_m < M
    offs_d = tl.arange(0, Z)
    d2 = offs_d[:, None]
    m2 = offs_m[None, :]
    base = pid_b*Z*M
    out_t = tl.load(out_ptr + base + d2*M + m2, mask=m_mask[None, :], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    mean = tl.sum(out_t, axis=0) / Z
    d = out_t - mean[None, :]
    var = tl.sum(d*d, axis=0) / Z
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)
    lw = tl.load(lw_ptr + offs_d)
    lb = tl.load(lb_ptr + offs_d)
    og_t = tl.load(og_ptr + base + d2*M + m2, mask=m_mask[None, :], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    norm_t = d * rstd[None, :] * lw[:, None] + lb[:, None]
    v = (norm_t * og_t).to(tl.float16)
    v_dot = tl.trans(v)
    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    for c0 in range(0, C_OUT, BLOCK_C):
       c_vals = c0 + c
       if C_NEEDS_MASK:
           cm = c_vals < C_OUT
           w = tl.load(w_ptr + offs_d[:, None] * C_STRIDE + c_vals[None, :], mask=cm[None, :], other=0.0)
           acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C), dtype=tl.float32)
           y = tl.dot(v_dot, w, acc)
           y_ptrs = y_ptr + (pid_b*M + offs_m)[:,None]*C_OUT + c_vals[None,:]
           tl.store(y_ptrs, y, mask=m_mask[:,None] & cm[None,:])
       else:
           w = tl.load(w_ptr + offs_d[:, None] * C_STRIDE + c_vals[None, :])
           acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C), dtype=tl.float32)
           y = tl.dot(v_dot, w, acc)
           y_ptrs = ...
           tl.store(y_ptrs, y, mask=m_mask[:,None])
```
If C_NEEDS_MASK false, but `C_OUT` might not be multiple of BLOCK_C? host ensures. Good. If C_NEEDS_MASK true, `range(0,C_OUT,BLOCK_C)` with BLOCK_C maybe > C_OUT? Python range yields 0 if start<stop? In Triton, range semantics likely same. If C_OUT=64, BLOCK_C=128, range(0,64,128) -> [0]. Good. But if C_OUT=0 not.

Potential issue: In C_NEEDS_MASK true, `w_ptr` row stride C_STRIDE=BLOCK_C, and c_vals beyond C but within BLOCK_C, mask other 0. Good.

Now, if C=128, BLOCK_C=128, C_NEEDS_MASK false. c0=0. Store mask valid. Good.

Now, if C=384, loop c0 0,128,256; c_vals tensor. For c0=256, c_vals 256..383. Good.

Now, host final weight if C%128==0: `wout = _trans_to_fp16(weights['to_out.weight'])` shape [Z,C]. `C_STRIDE=C`. If C%128 !=0: `C_BLOCK_F = _next_pow2_at_least_16(C)`, `wout = _trans_to_fp16(..., shape=(Z, C_BLOCK_F))`, `C_STRIDE=C_BLOCK_F`, `C_NEEDS_MASK=True`. Dot helpers for projection exact:
```
@triton.jit
def dot_proj1(xh, w_ptr, Z: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, C_IN: tl.constexpr):
    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    z = tl.arange(0, Z)
    w0 = tl.load(w_ptr + z[:, None] * C_IN + c[None, :])
    w = tl.trans(w0)
    acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, Z), dtype=tl.float32)
    return tl.dot(xh, w, acc)

@triton.jit
def dot_proj2(xh0,xh1,w_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C_IN):
   c = tl.arange(0,BLOCK_C)
   z = tl.arange(0,Z)
   w0 = tl.load(w_ptr + z[:,None]*C_IN + c[None,:])
   acc = zeros; acc=dot(xh0, trans(w0), acc)
   c1 = BLOCK_C + c
   w1 = load(... c1 ...)
   acc=dot(xh1, trans(w1), acc)
   return acc
```

Now, generic PyTorch projection uses `mask_flat` and `x`. Need be careful with memory and device. Code:
```
def _proj_pytorch(x, mask, weights, nw, nb, B,N,C,Z,M,has_mask):
    x2d = x.view(B, M, C).float()
    xn = torch.nn.functional.layer_norm(x2d, (C,), nw, nb, eps=1e-5)
    wl = weights['left_proj.weight'].float()
    ...
    left = torch.nn.functional.linear(xn, wl)
    ...
    if has_mask:
        m = mask.view(B, M).float().unsqueeze(-1)
        left = left * m * lg
        right = right * m * rg
    else:
        left = left * lg; right = right * rg
    og = og
    # transpose to d-major
    A = left.permute(0,2,1).reshape(B*Z, M).to(torch.float16).contiguous()
    Bm = right.permute(0,2,1).reshape(B*Z, M).to(torch.float16).contiguous()
    OG = og.permute(0,2,1).reshape(B*Z, M).to(torch.float16).contiguous()
    return A,Bm,OG
```

Another concern: In projection exact, `xh0 = (...).to(tl.float16)`. If norm weight/bias fp32, operations fp32. Good.

Now, final code length large but okay.

Let's write final answer.
</think>

```python
"""
Fused Triton implementation of TriMul forward pass.

Pipeline:
1. Fused LayerNorm + five linear projections + sigmoid gating in one kernel
   for exact channel sizes 128/256/384.  The gated left/right tensors and the
   output gate are written in [batch, hidden, seq, seq] layout to enable
   efficient tensor-core batched matmuls.
2. Batched N^3 contraction:
   out[b, d, i, j] = sum_k left[b, d, i, k] * right[b, d, j, k]
   implemented as a Triton tensor-core GEMM with fp16 inputs and fp32
   accumulation.
3. Fused final LayerNorm + output gate + output projection in Triton.

A small generic PyTorch branch is provided for out-of-list channel sizes to
keep the submission shape-robust; the measured/test configs use the fused
Triton projection path.
"""

import torch
import triton
import triton.language as tl
import torch.nn.functional as F


def _fp16_contig(w):
    return w.detach().to(torch.float16).contiguous()


def _fp32_contig(w):
    return w.detach().to(torch.float32).contiguous()


def _next_pow2_at_least_16(n):
    p = 16
    while p < n:
        p *= 2
    return p


@triton.jit
def _dot_proj1(
    xh,
    w_ptr,
    Z: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    C_IN: tl.constexpr,
):
    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    z = tl.arange(0, Z)
    # weight is stored as [Z, C_IN].  Load a tile [Z, BLOCK_C] and transpose
    # to [BLOCK_C, Z] for tl.dot.
    w = tl.load(w_ptr + z[:, None] * C_IN + c[None, :])
    acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, Z), dtype=tl.float32)
    return tl.dot(xh, tl.trans(w), acc)


@triton.jit
def _dot_proj2(
    xh0,
    xh1,
    w_ptr,
    Z: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    C_IN: tl.constexpr,
):
    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    z = tl.arange(0, Z)

    w0 = tl.load(w_ptr + z[:, None] * C_IN + c[None, :])
    acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, Z), dtype=tl.float32)
    acc = tl.dot(xh0, tl.trans(w0), acc)

    c1 = BLOCK_C + c
    w1 = tl.load(w_ptr + z[:, None] * C_IN + c1[None, :])
    acc = tl.dot(xh1, tl.trans(w1), acc)

    return acc


@triton.jit
def _dot_proj3(
    xh0,
    xh1,
    xh2,
    w_ptr,
    Z: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    C_IN: tl.constexpr,
):
    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    z = tl.arange(0, Z)

    w0 = tl.load(w_ptr + z[:, None] * C_IN + c[None, :])
    acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, Z), dtype=tl.float32)
    acc = tl.dot(xh0, tl.trans(w0), acc)

    c1 = BLOCK_C + c
    w1 = tl.load(w_ptr + z[:, None] * C_IN + c1[None, :])
    acc = tl.dot(xh1, tl.trans(w1), acc)

    c2 = 2 * BLOCK_C + c
    w2 = tl.load(w_ptr + z[:, None] * C_IN + c2[None, :])
    acc = tl.dot(xh2, tl.trans(w2), acc)

    return acc


@triton.jit
def _store_gated(
    pre,
    gate,
    out_ptr,
    base,
    M,
    Z: tl.constexpr,
    z,
    offs_m,
    store_mask,
    mask_b,
):
    val = pre * mask_b * (1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-gate)))
    ptrs = out_ptr + base + z[:, None] * M + offs_m[None, :]
    tl.store(ptrs, tl.trans(val).to(tl.float16), mask=store_mask)


@triton.jit
def _store_sigmoid(
    val,
    out_ptr,
    base,
    M,
    Z: tl.constexpr,
    z,
    offs_m,
    store_mask,
):
    v = 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-val))
    ptrs = out_ptr + base + z[:, None] * M + offs_m[None, :]
    tl.store(ptrs, tl.trans(v).to(tl.float16), mask=store_mask)


@triton.jit
def _proj_kernel(
    x_ptr,
    mask_ptr,
    a_ptr,
    b_ptr,
    og_ptr,
    nw_ptr,
    nb_ptr,
    wl_ptr,
    wlg_ptr,
    wr_ptr,
    wrg_ptr,
    wog_ptr,
    M,
    C: tl.constexpr,
    Z: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid_b = tl.program_id(0)
    pid_m = tl.program_id(1)

    offs_m = pid_m * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    m_mask = offs_m < M
    x_base = x_ptr + (pid_b * M + offs_m)[:, None] * C

    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    z = tl.arange(0, Z)
    base = pid_b * Z * M
    store_mask = (offs_m < M)[None, :]

    if HAS_MASK:
        mask_vec = tl.load(
            mask_ptr + pid_b * M + offs_m,
            mask=m_mask,
            other=0.0,
        ).to(tl.float32)
        mask_b = mask_vec[:, None]
    else:
        mask_b = 1.0

    if C == BLOCK_C:
        x0 = tl.load(x_base + c[None, :], mask=m_mask[:, None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

        mean = tl.sum(x0, axis=1) / C
        d0 = x0 - mean[:, None]
        var = tl.sum(d0 * d0, axis=1) / C
        rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)

        rw = tl.load(nw_ptr + c)
        rb = tl.load(nb_ptr + c)
        xh0 = (d0 * rstd[:, None] * rw[None, :] + rb[None, :]).to(tl.float16)

        lp = _dot_proj1(xh0, wl_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        lg = _dot_proj1(xh0, wlg_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        _store_gated(lp, lg, a_ptr, base, M, Z, z, offs_m, store_mask, mask_b)

        rp = _dot_proj1(xh0, wr_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        rg = _dot_proj1(xh0, wrg_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        _store_gated(rp, rg, b_ptr, base, M, Z, z, offs_m, store_mask, mask_b)

        og = _dot_proj1(xh0, wog_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        _store_sigmoid(og, og_ptr, base, M, Z, z, offs_m, store_mask)

    elif C == 2 * BLOCK_C:
        c1 = BLOCK_C + c

        x0 = tl.load(x_base + c[None, :], mask=m_mask[:, None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        x1 = tl.load(x_base + c1[None, :], mask=m_mask[:, None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

        sum_x = tl.sum(x0, axis=1) + tl.sum(x1, axis=1)
        mean = sum_x / C
        d0 = x0 - mean[:, None]
        d1 = x1 - mean[:, None]
        var = (tl.sum(d0 * d0, axis=1) + tl.sum(d1 * d1, axis=1)) / C
        rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)

        rw0 = tl.load(nw_ptr + c)
        rb0 = tl.load(nb_ptr + c)
        rw1 = tl.load(nw_ptr + c1)
        rb1 = tl.load(nb_ptr + c1)

        xh0 = (d0 * rstd[:, None] * rw0[None, :] + rb0[None, :]).to(tl.float16)
        xh1 = (d1 * rstd[:, None] * rw1[None, :] + rb1[None, :]).to(tl.float16)

        lp = _dot_proj2(xh0, xh1, wl_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        lg = _dot_proj2(xh0, xh1, wlg_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        _store_gated(lp, lg, a_ptr, base, M, Z, z, offs_m, store_mask, mask_b)

        rp = _dot_proj2(xh0, xh1, wr_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        rg = _dot_proj2(xh0, xh1, wrg_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        _store_gated(rp, rg, b_ptr, base, M, Z, z, offs_m, store_mask, mask_b)

        og = _dot_proj2(xh0, xh1, wog_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        _store_sigmoid(og, og_ptr, base, M, Z, z, offs_m, store_mask)

    elif C == 3 * BLOCK_C:
        c1 = BLOCK_C + c
        c2 = 2 * BLOCK_C + c

        x0 = tl.load(x_base + c[None, :], mask=m_mask[:, None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        x1 = tl.load(x_base + c1[None, :], mask=m_mask[:, None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        x2 = tl.load(x_base + c2[None, :], mask=m_mask[:, None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

        sum_x = tl.sum(x0, axis=1) + tl.sum(x1, axis=1) + tl.sum(x2, axis=1)
        mean = sum_x / C

        d0 = x0 - mean[:, None]
        d1 = x1 - mean[:, None]
        d2 = x2 - mean[:, None]

        var = (
            tl.sum(d0 * d0, axis=1)
            + tl.sum(d1 * d1, axis=1)
            + tl.sum(d2 * d2, axis=1)
        ) / C
        rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)

        rw0 = tl.load(nw_ptr + c)
        rb0 = tl.load(nb_ptr + c)
        rw1 = tl.load(nw_ptr + c1)
        rb1 = tl.load(nb_ptr + c1)
        rw2 = tl.load(nw_ptr + c2)
        rb2 = tl.load(nb_ptr + c2)

        xh0 = (d0 * rstd[:, None] * rw0[None, :] + rb0[None, :]).to(tl.float16)
        xh1 = (d1 * rstd[:, None] * rw1[None, :] + rb1[None, :]).to(tl.float16)
        xh2 = (d2 * rstd[:, None] * rw2[None, :] + rb2[None, :]).to(tl.float16)

        lp = _dot_proj3(xh0, xh1, xh2, wl_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        lg = _dot_proj3(xh0, xh1, xh2, wlg_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        _store_gated(lp, lg, a_ptr, base, M, Z, z, offs_m, store_mask, mask_b)

        rp = _dot_proj3(xh0, xh1, xh2, wr_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        rg = _dot_proj3(xh0, xh1, xh2, wrg_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        _store_gated(rp, rg, b_ptr, base, M, Z, z, offs_m, store_mask, mask_b)

        og = _dot_proj3(xh0, xh1, xh2, wog_ptr, Z, BLOCK_C, BLOCK_M, C)
        _store_sigmoid(og, og_ptr, base, M, Z, z, offs_m, store_mask)


@triton.jit
def _contract_kernel(
    a_ptr,
    b_ptr,
    c_ptr,
    M,
    N,
    BLOCK_I: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_J: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_K: tl.constexpr,
    EVEN: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid_i = tl.program_id(0)
    pid_j = tl.program_id(1)
    pid_d = tl.program_id(2)

    offs_i = pid_i * BLOCK_I + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_I)
    offs_j = pid_j * BLOCK_J + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_J)
    offs_k = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_K)

    base = pid_d * M

    if EVEN:
        a_ptrs = a_ptr + base + offs_i[:, None] * N + offs_k[None, :]
        b_ptrs = b_ptr + base + offs_j[:, None] * N + offs_k[None, :]

        acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_I, BLOCK_J), dtype=tl.float32)
        for _ in range(0, tl.cdiv(N, BLOCK_K)):
            a = tl.load(a_ptrs)
            b = tl.load(b_ptrs)
            acc = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(b), acc)
            a_ptrs += BLOCK_K
            b_ptrs += BLOCK_K

        c_ptrs = c_ptr + base + offs_i[:, None] * N + offs_j[None, :]
        tl.store(c_ptrs, acc.to(tl.float16))
    else:
        i_mask = offs_i < N
        j_mask = offs_j < N

        acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_I, BLOCK_J), dtype=tl.float32)
        for k in range(0, tl.cdiv(N, BLOCK_K)):
            kk = k * BLOCK_K + offs_k
            k_mask = kk < N

            a = tl.load(
                a_ptr + base + offs_i[:, None] * N + kk[None, :],
                mask=i_mask[:, None] & k_mask[None, :],
                other=0.0,
            )
            b = tl.load(
                b_ptr + base + offs_j[:, None] * N + kk[None, :],
                mask=j_mask[:, None] & k_mask[None, :],
                other=0.0,
            )
            acc = tl.dot(a, tl.trans(b), acc)

        c_ptrs = c_ptr + base + offs_i[:, None] * N + offs_j[None, :]
        tl.store(c_ptrs, acc.to(tl.float16), mask=i_mask[:, None] & j_mask[None, :])


@triton.jit
def _final_kernel(
    out_ptr,
    og_ptr,
    w_ptr,
    lw_ptr,
    lb_ptr,
    y_ptr,
    M,
    C_OUT: tl.constexpr,
    Z: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
    C_NEEDS_MASK: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid_b = tl.program_id(0)
    pid_m = tl.program_id(1)

    offs_m = pid_m * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    m_mask = offs_m < M

    offs_d = tl.arange(0, Z)
    d2 = offs_d[:, None]
    m2 = offs_m[None, :]

    base = pid_b * Z * M

    # Load [Z, BLOCK_M] so that the token dimension is the contiguous axis.
    out_t = tl.load(out_ptr + base + d2 * M + m2, mask=m_mask[None, :], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    og_t = tl.load(og_ptr + base + d2 * M + m2, mask=m_mask[None, :], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

    mean = tl.sum(out_t, axis=0) / Z
    diff = out_t - mean[None, :]
    var = tl.sum(diff * diff, axis=0) / Z
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)

    lw = tl.load(lw_ptr + offs_d)
    lb = tl.load(lb_ptr + offs_d)

    norm_t = diff * rstd[None, :] * lw[:, None] + lb[:, None]
    v = (norm_t * og_t).to(tl.float16)
    v_dot = tl.trans(v)  # [BLOCK_M, Z]

    c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)

    for c0 in range(0, C_OUT, BLOCK_C):
        c_vals = c0 + c

        if C_NEEDS_MASK:
            cm = c_vals < C_OUT
            # W is stored as [C_OUT, Z].  Load [BLOCK_C, Z], then transpose to [Z, BLOCK_C].
            w_orig = tl.load(
                w_ptr + c_vals[:, None] * Z + offs_d[None, :],
                mask=cm[:, None],
                other=0.0,
            )
            w = tl.trans(w_orig)
            acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C), dtype=tl.float32)
            y = tl.dot(v_dot, w, acc)
            y_ptrs = y_ptr + (pid_b * M + offs_m)[:, None] * C_OUT + c_vals[None, :]
            tl.store(y_ptrs, y, mask=m_mask[:, None] & cm[None, :])
        else:
            w_orig = tl.load(w_ptr + c_vals[:, None] * Z + offs_d[None, :])
            w = tl.trans(w_orig)
            acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C), dtype=tl.float32)
            y = tl.dot(v_dot, w, acc)
            y_ptrs = y_ptr + (pid_b * M + offs_m)[:, None] * C_OUT + c_vals[None, :]
            tl.store(y_ptrs, y, mask=m_mask[:, None])


def _projection_pytorch(x, mask, weights, B, M, C, Z, has_mask):
    """
    Correct generic projection path for channel sizes not covered by the
    specialized fused Triton projection.  The returned tensors use the same
    [B*Z, M] fp16 layout required by the Triton contraction/final kernels.
    """
    x2d = x.view(B, M, C).float()
    xn = F.layer_norm(
        x2d,
        (C,),
        weights["norm.weight"].float(),
        weights["norm.bias"].float(),
        eps=1e-5,
    )

    wl = weights["left_proj.weight"].float()
    wr = weights["right_proj.weight"].float()
    wlg = weights["left_gate.weight"].float()
    wrg = weights["right_gate.weight"].float()
    wog = weights["out_gate.weight"].float()

    left = F.linear(xn, wl)
    right = F.linear(xn, wr)
    lg = F.linear(xn, wlg).sigmoid()
    rg = F.linear(xn, wrg).sigmoid()
    og = F.linear(xn, wog).sigmoid()

    left = left * lg
    right = right * rg

    if has_mask:
        m = mask.view(B, M).float().unsqueeze(-1)
        left = left * m
        right = right * m

    a = left.permute(0, 2, 1).reshape(B * Z, M).to(torch.float16).contiguous()
    b = right.permute(0, 2, 1).reshape(B * Z, M).to(torch.float16).contiguous()
    g = og.permute(0, 2, 1).reshape(B * Z, M).to(torch.float16).contiguous()
    return a, b, g


def custom_kernel(data):
    if len(data) == 3:
        x, weights, config = data
        mask = None
    else:
        x, mask, weights, config = data

    B, N, _, C = x.shape
    Z = int(config.get("hidden_dim", 128))
    M = N * N
    device = x.device

    x = x.contiguous()

    has_mask = (mask is not None) and (not config.get("nomask", False))
    if has_mask:
        mask = mask.contiguous().reshape(B, M)

    norm_w = _fp32_contig(weights["norm.weight"])
    norm_b = _fp32_contig(weights["norm.bias"])
    out_norm_w = _fp32_contig(weights["to_out_norm.weight"])
    out_norm_b = _fp32_contig(weights["to_out_norm.bias"])

    use_fused_proj = C in (128, 256, 384)

    if use_fused_proj:
        BLOCK_M = 128 if C == 128 else 64
        BLOCK_C = 128

        left_w = _fp16_contig(weights["left_proj.weight"])
        right_w = _fp16_contig(weights["right_proj.weight"])
        left_g_w = _fp16_contig(weights["left_gate.weight"])
        right_g_w = _fp16_contig(weights["right_gate.weight"])
        out_g_w = _fp16_contig(weights["out_gate.weight"])
        out_w = _fp16_contig(weights["to_out.weight"])

        A = torch.empty((B * Z, M), dtype=torch.float16, device=device)
        Bm = torch.empty((B * Z, M), dtype=torch.float16, device=device)
        OG = torch.empty((B * Z, M), dtype=torch.float16, device=device)

        grid = (B, triton.cdiv(M, BLOCK_M))
        _proj_kernel[grid](
            x,
            mask if has_mask else x,
            A,
            Bm,
            OG,
            norm_w,
            norm_b,
            left_w,
            left_g_w,
            right_w,
            right_g_w,
            out_g_w,
            M,
            C=C,
            Z=Z,
            BLOCK_M=BLOCK_M,
            BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C,
            HAS_MASK=has_mask,
            num_warps=8,
            num_stages=2,
        )
    else:
        A, Bm, OG = _projection_pytorch(x, mask, weights, B, M, C, Z, has_mask)
        out_w = _fp16_contig(weights["to_out.weight"])

    # Contraction: out[b, d, i, j] = sum_k A[b, d, i, k] * B[b, d, j, k]
    Ctr = torch.empty((B * Z, M), dtype=torch.float16, device=device)

    BLOCK_I = 128
    BLOCK_J = 128
    BLOCK_K = 64
    num_i = triton.cdiv(N, BLOCK_I)
    num_j = triton.cdiv(N, BLOCK_J)
    even = (N % BLOCK_I == 0) and (N % BLOCK_J == 0) and (N % BLOCK_K == 0)

    contract_grid = (num_i, num_j, B * Z)
    _contract_kernel[contract_grid](
        A,
        Bm,
        Ctr,
        M,
        N,
        BLOCK_I=BLOCK_I,
        BLOCK_J=BLOCK_J,
        BLOCK_K=BLOCK_K,
        EVEN=even,
        num_warps=8,
        num_stages=3,
    )

    # Final fused LN + gate + projection.
    BLOCK_M_FINAL = 128 if C <= 128 else 64

    if C % 128 == 0:
        BLOCK_C_FINAL = 128
        c_needs_mask = False
    else:
        BLOCK_C_FINAL = _next_pow2_at_least_16(C)
        if BLOCK_C_FINAL != C:
            tmp = torch.zeros((BLOCK_C_FINAL, Z), dtype=torch.float16, device=device)
            tmp[:C, :] = out_w
            out_w = tmp
            c_needs_mask = True
        else:
            c_needs_mask = False

    y = torch.empty((B, N, N, C), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)

    final_grid = (B, triton.cdiv(M, BLOCK_M_FINAL))
    _final_kernel[final_grid](
        Ctr,
        OG,
        out_w,
        out_norm_w,
        out_norm_b,
        y,
        M,
        C_OUT=C,
        Z=Z,
        BLOCK_M=BLOCK_M_FINAL,
        BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C_FINAL,
        C_NEEDS_MASK=c_needs_mask,
        num_warps=8,
        num_stages=3,
    )

    return y
```
